([sandbaseai/workbuddy-skill](https://github.com/sandbaseai/workbuddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Verification gate before any claim of success. Requires fresh output from the command that proves each claim (tests, build, type-check, lint, reproduction of the original symptom, benchmark), and honest reporting of anything not verified. ([milad-raeisi/senior-skills](https://github.com/milad-raeisi/senior-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims) · ★4)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Use immediately before claiming that work is complete, fixed, passing, safe, merge-ready, or otherwise verified. ([HaffASE/CodexAtelier](https://github.com/HaffASE/CodexAtelier/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims) · ★3)
- **evidence-before-claims**: Use before claiming ANYTHING works, passes, is fixed, or is done — before every commit, PR, task hand-off, or "it should work now". Triggers on the urge to say done, or words like "should", "probably", "that fixes it", "all green". - **evidence-before-claims**: Discipline. Verify before saying something is true, and say what wasn't verified. ([rbadillap/sre](https://github.com/rbadillap/sre/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-before-claims))
- **evidence-before-claims**: Checks version-dependent and external facts against a source before stating them: APIs, flags, defaults, standards, statutes, subagent findings, and the status, authorship and security claims a README or spec makes before a push. Labels each claim verified, reported or inferred. Use when an answer depends on a dependency or API, even when it seems known, before citing a CVE, RFC or law, or before docs are pushed. Not for logic bugs. ([Rene-Roscher/skills](https://github.com/Rene-Roscher/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/evidence-before-claims))
- **evidence-before-completion**: Use immediately before claiming work is complete, tests pass, builds succeed, or bugs are fixed — run the real verification commands in this session and cite outcomes. - **evidence-before-done**: 多助手协作底座。任何助手准备说「做好了」「修好了」「放好了」「没问题」「都对上了」， 或要交付、在进度里说某部分已完成、进入下一步、转述别的助手或子任务的「已完成」之前用： 先拿到对得上当前版本、范围、环境的有效证据（打开看、播放、重算、按原步骤再走一遍、逐条对照原要求）； 本人或指定核验人对同一版本的有效核验可复用，有变更或失败只补核受影响部分；执行人自己的报告不算独立核验。 只报进行到哪的普通进度不触发全套重验。证据撑得住才下结论，撑不住就照实报现状。 不用于：第三方独立复核清单（另用审核类技能）； 动手前讲方案（另用方案确认类技能）。与同仓 ../dispatch-accept-verify/ 分工：那支管收回来，本支管核过再宣称完成。 ([dabingfy-dot/solo-to-team-skills](https://github.com/dabingfy-dot/solo-to-team-skills/tree/HEAD/bundles/bundle1/packages/Solo-to-Team-deepseek-bundle1/foundation/skills/evidence-before-done) · ★1.1k · 5 copies)

- **evidence-evaluation**: Systematic evidence-quality assessment using the CRAAP framework. - **evidence-evaluation**: Ocenia siłę zebranych dowodów, analizuje kontekst (w tym wykrywa manipulacje kontekstowe), wylicza wskaźniki wiarygodności i niezależnych potwierdzeń oraz identyfikuje sprzeczności między źródłami. - **evidence-evaluation**: 【事实与材料整理类】对证据材料评估真实性、合法性、关联性（三性）及证明力。Use when 提交证据后判断能否被采纳、对方质证需回应、全案证据综合评判待证事实、判断是否达到证明标准（高度盖然性/排除合理怀疑）、识别证据链薄弱环节并提出补强建议，或评估电子数据、鉴定意见、证人证言等特殊证据。 ([sy220284/999](https://github.com/sy220284/999/tree/HEAD/skills/evidence-evaluation))

- **evidence-first**: Main coding workflow for any task that writes, modifies, fixes or refactors code - features, bug fixes, refactors, config changes, even one-line edits ("fix X", "add Y", "implement", "refactor", "make it do Z"). Sizes the task, records a baseline, logs every check as a real tool call in a ledger, has a reviewer on a different model attack medium and large changes, and presents an evidence bundle. - **evidence-first**: Find the receipt before proposing the work. - **evidence-first**: The master workflow that ties the Nexrall reliability and security skills together. - **evidence-first**: Prima di prendere decisioni progettuali importanti o modificare significativamente un prodotto, fai ricerca mirata sulle questioni che non possono essere risolte con semplice buon senso o requisiti espliciti. Usa questa skill per UX, UI, architettura dell'informazione, conversione, copy, pricing, onboarding, game design, sistemi di progressione/economia e altre decisioni che influenzano significativamente utenti o risultati del progetto.
